# U4 requirements map

## Published-basis reference comparison

Source-specific lot averages, representative data, research measurements and bounds are not all coupon means. These are reference comparisons, not conservative design allowables or guaranteed physical bounds. Different floor winners are not one simultaneously attainable material. F0/P0 omitted masses, Klocal ASSUMED and hierarchy HEURISTIC persist.

Qualified working Y = UNKNOWN_UNADOPTED; working gap = UNKNOWN throughout.

Each scenario retains its six span aliases without duplicated span physics. The complete machine companions preserve the original row and every candidate's result or exclusion.

typical values are coupon means, so the gap is a best case, not a conservative one.

Signed gap = 100 × (X required / Q reference − 1): positive shortfall, zero equality, negative surplus. Numerical tie/boundary flags use 1e-12 + 1e-10 × max absolute value at the stated SI scale; they are not uncertainty estimates.

### Hierarchy requirement floors

typical values are coupon means, so the gap is a best case, not a conservative one.

Source-specific lot averages, representative data, research measurements and bounds are not all coupon means. These are reference comparisons, not conservative design allowables or guaranteed physical bounds. Different floor winners are not one simultaneously attainable material. F0/P0 omitted masses, Klocal ASSUMED and hierarchy HEURISTIC persist.

Signed gap = 100 × (X required / Q reference − 1): positive shortfall, zero equality, negative surplus. Numerical tie/boundary flags use 1e-12 + 1e-10 × max absolute value at the stated SI scale; they are not uncertainty estimates.

### Reference bases and assumptions

Full original values, units, directions, methods, source version/locator/hash, density provenance, state gaps, eligibility and transformation assumptions follow for every retained basis. T-UD75 acts once on raw E; its variants have no compression allowance. Hy-Bor uses compression modulus190 GPa, not tensile200 GPa, and has no hoop/effective transform.
